[BUG] None\n**Describe the bug**\r\nI have a function which has a flag to indicate whether a progress bar should be shown or not.  By using the `visible` task kwarg I can effectively hide the progress bar while keeping the rest of the iteration code the same.\r\n\r\nHowever, when the progress bar is hidden, an empty line of output is created when it completes. I thought that setting `Progress(transient=True)` might suppress the extraneous new line.  In my code I use `Progress(transient = not show_progress)` but set it to always be `True` here for demonstration purposes.\r\n\r\nWhen `Progress(transient=True)` and `visible=True` in the task, the progress bar displayed and then correctly erased upon completion and there is no extra empty line. if file exists then skip download\r\n        if path.is_file():\r\n            raise SkipDownloadException\r\n\r\n        # do download\r\n\r\nclass Downloader:\r\n    \"\"\"Download files or webpages with rich progressbar.\"\"\"\r\n\r\n    @contextmanager\r\n    def begin(self):\r\n       \r\n        status: DownloaderExitStatus\r\n\r\n        with Progress(transient=True) as prog:\r\n            try:\r\n                yield DownloadContext(prog)\r\n            except SkipDownloadException:\r\n                status=DownloaderExitStatus.SUCCESS_SKIPPED\r\n            else:\r\n                status=DownloaderExitStatus.SUCCESS_DOWNLOAD\r\n    \r\n       #
